const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const COLORS = {low:'#06b6d4',moderate:'#22c55e',elevated:'#eab308',high:'#f97316',critical:'#ef4444'};

// === Advanced Preview v3.0 — 11 сигналов фаз J, K, L, M ===

const ADVANCED_SIGNAL_DEFS = [
  // Фаза J: причинность
  { id: 'causal_chain', phase: 'J', section: 'Причинность', icon: '🔗', label: 'Causal Chain', color: '#8b5cf6',
    extract: (d) => d.combinedCausalSignal ? {
      value: d.combinedCausalSignal.type,
      severity: d.combinedCausalSignal.severity,
      tooltip: d.combinedCausalSignal.interpretation,
      trend: null,
    } : null },
  { id: 'lag_shift', phase: 'J', section: 'Причинность', icon: '⚡', label: 'Lag Shifts', color: '#f97316',
    extract: (d) => {
      const shifts = d.temporalCausal?.shiftDetections || [];
      const accelerating = shifts.filter(s => s.type === 'accelerating');
      if (accelerating.length === 0) return null;
      return {
        value: accelerating.length + ' ускор.',
        severity: accelerating.some(s => s.severity === 'high') ? 'critical' : 'medium',
        tooltip: accelerating.map(s => s.from + ' → ' + s.to + ': ' + (s.shiftPct > 0 ? '+' : '') + s.shiftPct.toFixed(0) + '%').join(' | '),
        trend: 'up',
      };
    } },
  { id: 'vulnerability', phase: 'J', section: 'Причинность', icon: '⚠️', label: 'Vulnerability', color: '#eab308',
    extract: (d) => {
      const v = d.temporalCausal?.vulnerabilityWindow;
      if (!v) return null;
      return {
        value: (v.vulnerability * 100).toFixed(0) + '% · ' + v.level,
        severity: v.level,
        tooltip: v.interpretation,
        trend: v.level === 'critical' || v.level === 'high' ? 'up' : 'down',
      };
    } },
  { id: 'cross_layer', phase: 'J', section: 'Причинность', icon: '🌐', label: 'Cross-Layer', color: '#06b6d4',
    extract: (d) => {
      const prop = d.multiLayerCausal?.propagation;
      if (!prop || prop.totalUpdates === 0) return null;
      const ratio = prop.crossLayerUpdates / prop.totalUpdates;
      return {
        value: prop.crossLayerUpdates + '/' + prop.totalUpdates + ' (' + (ratio * 100).toFixed(0) + '%)',
        severity: ratio > 0.5 ? 'high' : ratio > 0.3 ? 'medium' : 'low',
        tooltip: prop.crossLayerUpdates + ' кросс-слойных обновлений из ' + prop.totalUpdates,
        trend: ratio > 0.3 ? 'up' : null,
      };
    } },

  // Фаза K: инфовойна + истощение
  { id: 'narrative_warfare', phase: 'K', section: 'Инфовойна & Ресурсы', icon: '📢', label: 'Info Ops', color: '#ec4899',
    extract: (d) => {
      const nw = d.narrativeWarfare;
      if (!nw?.available || nw.campaignsDetected === 0) return null;
      return {
        value: nw.campaignsDetected + ' кампаний',
        severity: nw.threatLevel > 0.7 ? 'critical' : nw.threatLevel > 0.5 ? 'high' : 'medium',
        tooltip: nw.assessment + ' · ' + (nw.campaigns?.[0]?.classification || ''),
        trend: 'up',
      };
    } },
  { id: 'strategic_assessment', phase: 'K', section: 'Инфовойна & Ресурсы', icon: '⚔️', label: 'Strategic', color: '#ef4444',
    extract: (d) => d.combinedStrategicAssessment ? {
      value: d.combinedStrategicAssessment.type,
      severity: d.combinedStrategicAssessment.severity,
      tooltip: d.combinedStrategicAssessment.interpretation,
      trend: 'up',
    } : null },
  { id: 'resources', phase: 'K', section: 'Инфовойна & Ресурсы', icon: '⛽', label: 'Resources', color: '#f97316',
    extract: (d) => {
      const re = d.resourceExhaustion;
      if (!re?.overallStatus) return null;
      const militaryDays = re.military?.criticalResource?.daysUntilCritical;
      return {
        value: re.overallStatus,
        severity: re.overallStatus,
        tooltip: re.interpretation + (militaryDays ? ' · ' + militaryDays.toFixed(0) + 'д до критического' : ''),
        trend: re.overallPressure > 0.5 ? 'up' : null,
      };
    } },

  // Фаза L: meta-ensemble + сценарии
  { id: 'meta_regime', phase: 'L', section: 'Meta & Сценарии', icon: '🎯', label: 'Regime', color: '#3b82f6',
    extract: (d) => {
      const me = d.metaEnsemble;
      if (!me?.currentRegime) return null;
      const topModel = Object.entries(me.modelWeights || {}).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0];
      return {
        value: me.currentRegime,
        severity: me.currentRegime === 'crisis' ? 'critical' : me.currentRegime === 'elevated' ? 'high' : 'low',
        tooltip: me.interpretation + (topModel ? ' · top: ' + topModel[0] + ' (' + (topModel[1] * 100).toFixed(0) + '%)' : ''),
        trend: null,
      };
    } },
  { id: 'scenarios', phase: 'L', section: 'Meta & Сценарии', icon: '🌳', label: 'Top Scenario', color: '#22c55e',
    extract: (d) => {
      const sc = d.scenarios;
      if (!sc?.scenarios?.length) return null;
      const top = sc.scenarios[0];
      return {
        value: top.name + ' (' + (top.probability * 100).toFixed(0) + '%)',
        severity: top.severity,
        tooltip: top.description || '',
        trend: null,
      };
    } },

  // Фаза M: новые слои v3.0
  { id: 'hypergraph', phase: 'M', section: 'v3.0 Слои', icon: '🕸', label: 'Hypergraph', color: '#8b5cf6',
    extract: (d) => {
      const hg = d.hypergraphContagion;
      if (!hg?.stats) return null;
      const active = (hg.criticalHyperedges || []).length;
      return {
        value: hg.stats.hyperedgeCount + ' рёбер · ' + active + ' активных',
        severity: active > 2 ? 'critical' : active > 0 ? 'medium' : 'low',
        tooltip: 'Узлов: ' + hg.stats.nodeCount + ' · ср. размер гиперребра: ' + hg.stats.avgHyperedgeSize?.toFixed(2),
        trend: active > 0 ? 'up' : null,
      };
    } },
  { id: 'attention', phase: 'M', section: 'v3.0 Слои', icon: '👁', label: 'Attention', color: '#06b6d4',
    extract: (d) => {
      const ad = d.attentionDynamics;
      if (!ad?.totalTopics) return null;
      const explosive = (ad.topExplosive || []).filter(e => e.willExplode).length;
      return {
        value: ad.totalTopics + ' тем · ' + explosive + ' взрывных',
        severity: explosive > 1 ? 'critical' : explosive === 1 ? 'high' : 'low',
        tooltip: 'Сдвигов внимания: ' + (ad.shifts?.length || 0) + ' · total: ' + ad.totalAttention?.toFixed(1),
        trend: explosive > 0 ? 'up' : null,
      };
    } },
  { id: 'coevolution', phase: 'M', section: 'v3.0 Слои', icon: '⚔', label: 'Co-evolution', color: '#ef4444',
    extract: (d) => {
      const ace = d.adversarialCoEvolution;
      if (!ace?.opponentsCount) return null;
      const adapting = (ace.adaptations || []).filter(a => a.adaptation?.adapted).length;
      return {
        value: ace.opponentsCount + ' противн. · ' + adapting + ' адапт.',
        severity: adapting > 1 ? 'critical' : adapting === 1 ? 'high' : 'low',
        tooltip: ace.keyInsight || '',
        trend: adapting > 0 ? 'up' : null,
      };
    } },
];

function renderSignalRow(s) {
  const severityClass = s.severity === 'critical' ? 'critical' : '';
  const trendHtml = s.trend
    ? '<span class="adv-signal-trend ' + s.trend + '">' + (s.trend === 'up' ? '↑' : '↓') + '</span>'
    : '';
  const escapedTooltip = String(s.tooltip || '')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
  return (
    '<div class="adv-signal ' + severityClass + '" style="border-left-color:' + s.color + ';" data-tooltip="' + escapedTooltip + '">' +
    '<span class="adv-signal-icon">' + s.icon + '</span>' +
    '<div class="adv-signal-content">' +
    '<div class="adv-signal-label">' + s.label + '</div>' +
    '<div class="adv-signal-value" style="color:' + s.color + ';">' + s.value + trendHtml + '</div>' +
    '</div></div>'
  );
}

function renderAdvancedPreview(data) {
  const el = $('advanced-preview-content');
  const footer = $('advanced-footer');
  if (!el) return;

  const signals = [];
  for (const def of ADVANCED_SIGNAL_DEFS) {
    try {
      const extracted = def.extract(data);
      if (extracted) signals.push({ ...def, ...extracted });
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('[advanced-preview] Signal ' + def.id + ' extraction failed:', e.message);
    }
  }

  const sections = {};
  for (const sig of signals) {
    if (!sections[sig.section]) sections[sig.section] = [];
    sections[sig.section].push(sig);
  }

  if (signals.length === 0) {
    el.innerHTML =
      '<div class="adv-empty">' +
      'Advanced-модули неактивны в последнем прогнозе.<br>' +
      'Запустите pipeline с опциями по умолчанию.' +
      '<a href="/advanced.html">Открыть Advanced Analytics →</a>' +
      '</div>';
    if (footer) footer.style.display = 'none';
    return;
  }

  const html = Object.entries(sections).map(([sectionName, sigs]) =>
    '<div class="adv-section-label">' + sectionName + '</div>' +
    sigs.map(s => renderSignalRow(s)).join('')
  ).join('');
  el.innerHTML = html;

  const criticalCount = signals.filter(s => s.severity === 'critical' || s.severity === 'high').length;
  if (footer) {
    footer.style.display = 'flex';
    const dotEl = $('footer-status-dot');
    if (dotEl) {
      dotEl.className = 'adv-footer-dot' + (criticalCount > 2 ? ' critical' : criticalCount > 0 ? ' warning' : '');
    }
    const statusEl = $('footer-status-text');
    if (statusEl) statusEl.textContent = criticalCount > 0 ? criticalCount + ' критичных' : 'все стабильно';
    const refreshEl = $('footer-refresh');
    if (refreshEl && data.timestamp) {
      refreshEl.textContent = new Date(data.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString('ru-RU', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
    }
  }

  const badge = $('badge-advanced');
  if (badge) {
    if (criticalCount > 0) {
      badge.textContent = criticalCount > 9 ? '9+' : String(criticalCount);
      badge.style.display = 'flex';
    } else {
      badge.style.display = 'none';
    }
  }
}

// === Загрузка данных ===

async function load() {
  try {
    const res = await fetch('../runs/predictions/latest_forecast.json');
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + res.status);
    const data = await res.json();
    render(data);
  } catch (e) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.empty').forEach(el => el.textContent = 'Нет данных: ' + e.message);
  }
}

function render(data) {
  if (data.timestamp) $('last-update').textContent = new Date(data.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString('ru-RU');
  if (data.sweepId) $('sweep-id').textContent = 'sweep: ' + data.sweepId.substring(0, 12);

  // Top risks
  if (data.topRisks && data.topRisks.length > 0) {
    $('top-risks').innerHTML = data.topRisks.map(r => {
      const color = COLORS[r.level || 'moderate'] || '#3b82f6';
      const prob = r.probability || 0;
      return '<div class="event-row" style="border-left-color:' + color + '">' +
        '<div class="event-header"><span class="event-name">' + (r.name || r.id) + '</span>' +
        '<span class="event-prob" style="color:' + color + '">' + (prob * 100).toFixed(1) + '%</span></div>' +
        '<div class="bar"><div class="bar-fill" style="width:' + (prob * 100) + '%;background:' + color + '"></div></div>' +
        '</div>';
    }).join('');
  }

  // System state
  if (data.systemState) {
    const s = data.systemState;
    $('system-state').innerHTML = '<div style="font-size:20px;font-weight:700;color:var(--accent);margin-bottom:8px">' + (s.current || '—') + '</div>' +
      '<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-dim);font-family:var(--mono)">Markov next: ' + (s.markov?.next || '—') + '</div>';
  }

  // Brier
  if (data.calibration) {
    const brier = data.calibration.brierScores || {};
    const entries = Object.entries(brier).sort((a, b) => (a[1].score || 0) - (b[1].score || 0));
    if (entries.length > 0) {
      $('brier-info').innerHTML = entries.map(([src, b]) => {
        const score = b.score || 0;
        const color = score < 0.1 ? '#22c55e' : score < 0.2 ? '#eab308' : '#ef4444';
        return '<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:6px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);font-size:12px">' +
          '<span>' + src + '</span>' +
          '<span style="font-family:var(--mono);color:' + color + ';font-weight:700">' + score.toFixed(4) + '</span></div>';
      }).join('');
    }
  }

  // Advanced preview
  renderAdvancedPreview(data);
}

load();
setInterval(load, 60000);
</script>

</body>
</html>

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
// КОНФИГ
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════

const CONFIG = {
  apiBase: window.location.origin,
  wsUrl: `ws://${window.location.host}/agent`,
  pollIntervalMs: 5000,
  useWebSocket: false, // переключается при успешном подключении
};

const TOOLS = [
  { name: 'engine_full', category: 'base' },
  { name: 'regime_shift', category: 'base' },
  { name: 'explainability', category: 'base' },
  { name: 'neural_causal_discovery', category: 'v6' },
  { name: 'continual_learning', category: 'v6' },
  { name: 'causal_rl', category: 'v6' },
  { name: 'quantum_hypergraph', category: 'v6' },
  { name: 'zk_federated', category: 'v6' },
  { name: 'mcmc', category: 'catalog' },
  { name: 'physics_inspired', category: 'catalog' },
  { name: 'automl', category: 'catalog' },
  { name: 'anomaly_detection', category: 'catalog' },
  { name: 'graph_sage', category: 'catalog' },
  { name: 'actor_critic', category: 'catalog' },
  { name: 'world_model', category: 'v7' },
  { name: 'neural_ode', category: 'v7' },
  { name: 'dreamer', category: 'v7' },
  { name: 'continuous_causal', category: 'v7' },
  { name: 'simulation_engine', category: 'v7' },
];

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
// СОСТОЯНИЕ
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════

let ws = null;
let isProcessing = false;
let sessionCounter = 0;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
// WEB SOCKET
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════

function connectWS() {
  try {
    ws = new WebSocket(CONFIG.wsUrl);
    ws.onopen = () => {
      CONFIG.useWebSocket = true;
      updateStatus('ws', 'ok', 'WS');
      console.log('[agent] WebSocket подключён');
    };
    ws.onmessage = (ev) => {
      try {
        const msg = JSON.parse(ev.data);
        if (msg.type === 'agent_response') {
          renderAgentResponse(msg.data);
          isProcessing = false;
          updateSendButton();
        } else if (msg.type === 'agent_status') {
          updateSystemStatus(msg.data);
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('[agent] parse error:', e);
      }
    };
    ws.onclose = () => {
      CONFIG.useWebSocket = false;
      updateStatus('ws', 'warn', 'WS off');
      // Переподключение через 5 секунд
      setTimeout(connectWS, 5000);
    };
    ws.onerror = () => {
      CONFIG.useWebSocket = false;
    };
  } catch (e) {
    CONFIG.useWebSocket = false;
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
// HTTP API
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════

async function callAgentHTTP(query) {
  const sessionId = `ui_${Date.now()}_${++sessionCounter}`;
  try {
    const resp = await fetch(`${CONFIG.apiBase}/api/agent/query`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ query, sessionId }),
    });
    if (!resp.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${resp.status}`);
    return await resp.json();
  } catch (e) {
    return {
      error: `Не удалось вызвать agent: ${e.message}. Проверь что сервер /api/agent/query запущен.`,
      sessionId,
    };
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
// UI
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════

function updateStatus(name, state, label) {
  const dot = document.getElementById(`${name}-status`);
  const lbl = document.getElementById(`${name}-label`);
  if (dot) {
    dot.className = 'dot ' + state;
  }
  if (lbl && label) lbl.textContent = label;
}

function updateSystemStatus(data) {
  if (!data) return;
  const set = (id, v, cls) => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (!el) return;
    el.textContent = v;
    el.className = 'v' + (cls ? ' ' + cls : '');
  };
  if (data.llm !== undefined) set('ss-llm', data.llm ? 'online' : 'offline', data.llm ? 'ok' : 'err');
  if (data.tools !== undefined) set('ss-tools', String(data.tools), data.tools > 0 ? 'ok' : 'err');
  if (data.history !== undefined) set('ss-history', String(data.history), data.history >= 20 ? 'ok' : 'warn');
  if (data.snapshot !== undefined) set('ss-snapshot', data.snapshot ? 'found' : 'missing', data.snapshot ? 'ok' : 'warn');
}

function appendMessage(role, content, meta) {
  const msg = document.createElement('div');
  msg.className = `msg ${role}`;
  if (meta) {
    const metaEl = document.createElement('div');
    metaEl.className = 'meta';
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(meta)) {
      const span = document.createElement('span');
      span.className = `badge ${v}`;
      span.textContent = k;
      metaEl.appendChild(span);
    }
    msg.appendChild(metaEl);
  }
  const textEl = document.createElement('div');
  textEl.textContent = content;
  msg.appendChild(textEl);
  document.getElementById('messages').appendChild(msg);
  msg.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'end' });
  return msg;
}

function appendThinking() {
  const el = document.createElement('div');
  el.className = 'thinking';
  el.innerHTML = 'Agent <span class="dots"></span>';
  el.id = 'thinking-indicator';
  document.getElementById('messages').appendChild(el);
  el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'end' });
  return el;
}

function removeThinking() {
  const el = document.getElementById('thinking-indicator');
  if (el) el.remove();
}

function renderAgentResponse(data) {
  removeThinking();

  if (data.error) {
    appendMessage('agent', `❌ ${data.error}`, { error: 'err' });
    return;
  }

  const meta = {
    mode: data.mode || 'unknown',
    confidence: data.answer?.confidence || data.confidence || 'unknown',
    source: data.answer?.source || 'template',
  };

  const text = data.answer?.text || data.answer || data.text || '(нет ответа)';
  const msg = appendMessage('agent', text, meta);

  // Раскрываемые детали (план + executions)
  if (data.plan || data.executions) {
    const details = document.createElement('details');
    const summary = document.createElement('summary');
    summary.textContent = `Детали (${data.plan?.length || 0} шагов, ${data.executions?.length || 0} вызовов)`;
    details.appendChild(summary);

    const pre = document.createElement('pre');
    pre.textContent = JSON.stringify({
      mode: data.mode,
      reasoning: data.reasoning,
      plan: data.plan,
      executions: data.executions?.map(e => ({
        tool: e.tool,
        args: e.args,
        ok: e.ok,
        error: e.error || null,
        elapsedMs: e.elapsedMs,
        resultKeys: e.result ? Object.keys(e.result) : null,
      })),
    }, null, 2);
    details.appendChild(pre);
    msg.appendChild(details);
  }
}

function updateSendButton() {
  const btn = document.getElementById('send');
  btn.disabled = isProcessing;
  btn.textContent = isProcessing ? 'Обработка...' : 'Отправить';
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ОТПРАВКА
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════

async function sendQuery() {
  const input = document.getElementById('input');
  const query = input.value.trim();
  if (!query || isProcessing) return;

  appendMessage('user', query);
  input.value = '';
  isProcessing = true;
  updateSendButton();
  appendThinking();

  if (CONFIG.useWebSocket && ws && ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
    ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'query', query }));
  } else {
    const result = await callAgentHTTP(query);
    renderAgentResponse(result);
    isProcessing = false;
    updateSendButton();
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ИНИЦИАЛИЗАЦИЯ
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════

function renderToolList() {
  const container = document.getElementById('tool-list');
  container.innerHTML = '';
  for (const tool of TOOLS) {
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'tool';
    el.innerHTML = `<span class="cat ${tool.category}">${tool.category}</span>${tool.name}`;
    container.appendChild(el);
  }
}

async function pollStatus() {
  try {
    const resp = await fetch(`${CONFIG.apiBase}/api/agent/status`);
    if (!resp.ok) throw new Error('status failed');
    const data = await resp.json();
    updateSystemStatus(data);
    updateStatus('llm', data.llm ? 'ok' : 'err', data.llm ? 'LLM online' : 'LLM off');
    updateStatus('history', data.history >= 20 ? 'ok' : 'warn', `History: ${data.history}`);
    updateStatus('snapshot', data.snapshot ? 'ok' : 'warn', data.snapshot ? 'Snapshot found' : 'No snapshot');
  } catch (e) {
    updateStatus('llm', 'warn', 'API off');
    updateStatus('history', 'warn', 'API off');
    updateStatus('snapshot', 'warn', 'API off');
  }
}

function init() {
  renderToolList();
  pollStatus();
  setInterval(pollStatus, CONFIG.pollIntervalMs);
  connectWS();

  const input = document.getElementById('input');
  input.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
      e.preventDefault();
      sendQuery();
    }
  });

  document.getElementById('send').addEventListener('click', sendQuery);

  document.querySelectorAll('.quick-btn').forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      input.value = btn.dataset.q;
      sendQuery();
    });
  });

  // Автовысота textarea
  input.addEventListener('input', () => {
    input.style.height = 'auto';
    input.style.height = Math.min(150, input.scrollHeight) + 'px';
  });
}

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
</script>
</body>
</html>

const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);

async function load() {
try {
const res = await fetch('../runs/predictions/latest_forecast.json');
if (!res.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + res.status);
const data = await res.json();
render(data);
} catch (e) {
document.querySelectorAll('.empty').forEach(el => el.textContent = 'Нет данных: ' + e.message);
}
}

function render(data) {
if (data.timestamp) $('last-update').textContent = new Date(data.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString('ru-RU');
if (data.metaEnsemble?.currentRegime) $('regime').textContent = 'regime: ' + data.metaEnsemble.currentRegime;

// Stats
$('stat-causal').textContent = data.combinedCausalSignal?.type || '—';
$('stat-warfare').textContent = data.narrativeWarfare?.campaignsDetected || 0;
$('stat-resources').textContent = data.resourceExhaustion?.overallStatus || '—';
$('stat-scenarios').textContent = data.scenarios?.scenarioCount || 0;

// Temporal
if (data.temporalCausal) {
const tc = data.temporalCausal;
const shifts = tc.shiftDetections || [];
$('temporal').innerHTML = '<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-dim);margin-bottom:8px">Событий: ' + (tc.totalEvents || 0) + ' · Сдвигов: ' + shifts.length + '</div>' +
(shifts.length > 0 ? shifts.slice(0, 5).map(s =>
'<div class="row" style="border-left-color:' + (s.type === 'accelerating' ? '#ef4444' : '#06b6d4') + '">' +
'<div class="row-header"><span class="row-name">' + s.from + ' → ' + s.to + '</span>' +
'<span class="row-value" style="color:' + (s.type === 'accelerating' ? '#ef4444' : '#06b6d4') + '">' + s.shiftPct.toFixed(0) + '%</span></div>' +
'<div class="row-meta">' + s.type + ' · z=' + s.zScore.toFixed(2) + '</div></div>'
).join('') : '<div class="empty">Нет сдвигов</div>');
}

// Multi-layer
if (data.multiLayerCausal) {
const ml = data.multiLayerCausal;
const prop = ml.propagation || {};
$('multilayer').innerHTML = '<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-dim);margin-bottom:8px">Узлов: ' + (ml.graphStats?.nodeCount || 0) + ' · Кросс-слойных: ' + (ml.graphStats?.crossLayerEdgeCount || 0) + '</div>' +
'<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-dim)">Propagation updates: ' + (prop.totalUpdates || 0) + ' (cross-layer: ' + (prop.crossLayerUpdates || 0) + ')</div>';
}

// Warfare
if (data.narrativeWarfare) {
const nw = data.narrativeWarfare;
$('warfare').innerHTML = '<div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--accent3);margin-bottom:8px">' + (nw.assessment || '—') + '</div>' +
'<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-dim)">Кампаний: ' + (nw.campaignsDetected || 0) + ' · Угроза: ' + ((nw.threatLevel || 0) * 100).toFixed(0) + '%</div>';
}

// Resources
if (data.resourceExhaustion) {
const re = data.resourceExhaustion;
$('resources').innerHTML = '<div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:8px">' + (re.overallStatus || '—') + '</div>' +
'<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-dim)">Давление: ' + ((re.overallPressure || 0) * 100).toFixed(0) + '%</div>' +
(re.interpretation ? '<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--text-faint);margin-top:6px">' + re.interpretation + '</div>' : '');
}

// Meta
if (data.metaEnsemble) {
const me = data.metaEnsemble;
const weights = Object.entries(me.modelWeights || {}).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 5);
$('meta').innerHTML = '<div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--accent);margin-bottom:8px">Режим: ' + (me.currentRegime || '—') + '</div>' +
weights.map(([name, w]) =>
'<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:4px 0;font-size:11px">' +
'<span>' + name + '</span><span style="font-family:var(--mono);color:var(--accent)">' + (w * 100).toFixed(1) + '%</span></div>'
).join('');
}

// Scenarios
if (data.scenarios?.scenarios) {
$('scenarios').innerHTML = data.scenarios.scenarios.slice(0, 6).map(sc =>
'<div class="row">' +
'<div class="row-header"><span class="row-name">' + sc.name + '</span>' +
'<span class="row-value">' + ((sc.probability || 0) * 100).toFixed(0) + '%</span></div>' +
'<div class="row-meta">' + sc.severity + ' · ' + (sc.horizonHours || '?') + 'ч</div></div>'
).join('');
}
}

load();
setInterval(load, 60000);

      <div style="background: var(--surface2); padding: 12px; border-radius: 10px; text-align: center;">
        <div style="font-family: var(--mono); font-size: 24px; font-weight: 800; color: var(--cyan);" id="stat-topics">—</div>
        <div style="font-size: 10px; color: var(--text-faint); text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.5px; margin-top: 4px;">Тем</div>
      </div>
      <div style="background: var(--surface2); padding: 12px; border-radius: 10px; text-align: center;">
        <div style="font-family: var(--mono); font-size: 24px; font-weight: 800; color: var(--cyan);" id="stat-total">—</div>
        <div style="font-size: 10px; color: var(--text-faint); text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.5px; margin-top: 4px;">Общее внимание</div>
      </div>
      <div style="background: var(--surface2); padding: 12px; border-radius: 10px; text-align: center;">
        <div style="font-family: var(--mono); font-size: 24px; font-weight: 800; color: var(--green);" id="stat-shifts">—</div>
        <div style="font-size: 10px; color: var(--text-faint); text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.5px; margin-top: 4px;">Сдвигов</div>
      </div>
      <div style="background: var(--surface2); padding: 12px; border-radius: 10px; text-align: center;">
        <div style="font-family: var(--mono); font-size: 24px; font-weight: 800; color: var(--red);" id="stat-explosive">—</div>
        <div style="font-size: 10px; color: var(--text-faint); text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.5px; margin-top: 4px;">Взрывных тем</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Allocation chart -->
  <div class="card col-6">
    <div class="card-title">
      Распределение внимания
      <span class="tag">доля каждой темы</span>

  <!-- Explosive topics -->

    <div class="card-title">
      Сдвиги внимания
      <span class="tag">от одной темы к другой</span>

<div class="tooltip" id="tooltip"></div>

<script src="https://d3js.org/d3.v7.min.js"></script>
<script>
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const CONFIG = { dataUrl: '../runs/predictions/latest_forecast.json', refreshMs: 60000 };

const COLORS = [
  '#3b82f6', '#8b5cf6', '#ec4899', '#ef4444', '#f97316',
  '#eab308', '#22c55e', '#06b6d4', '#14b8a6', '#a855f7',
  '#f43f5e', '#84cc16', '#0ea5e9', '#6366f1', '#d946ef',
];

let DATA = null;

async function load() {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(CONFIG.dataUrl);
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
    DATA = await res.json();
    render();
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Load error:', e);
  }
}

function render() {
  if (!DATA) return;
  const ad = DATA.attentionDynamics;
  if (!ad) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.empty').forEach(el => el.textContent = 'Нет данных об attention dynamics');
    return;
  }

  renderStats(ad);
  renderAllocation(ad);
  renderExplosive(ad);
  renderTimeline(ad);
  renderShifts(ad);
}

function renderStats(ad) {
  $('stat-topics').textContent = ad.totalTopics || 0;
  $('stat-total').textContent = (ad.totalAttention || 0).toFixed(1);
  $('stat-shifts').textContent = ad.shifts?.length || 0;
  $('stat-explosive').textContent = (ad.topExplosive || []).filter(e => e.willExplode).length;
}

function renderAllocation(ad) {
  const svg = $('allocation-chart');
  const w = svg.clientWidth || 600;
  const h = 400;
  svg.setAttribute('viewBox', `0 0 ${w} ${h}`);
  svg.innerHTML = '';

  const alloc = ad.attentionAllocation || {};
  const entries = Object.entries(alloc)
    .map(([id, data]) => ({ id, ...data }))
    .sort((a, b) => b.share - a.share)
    .slice(0, 15);

  if (entries.length === 0) {
    svg.innerHTML = `<text x="${w/2}" y="${h/2}" text-anchor="middle" fill="#5a6b8a" font-size="12">Нет данных</text>`;
    return;
  }

  const radius = Math.min(w, h) / 2 - 60;
  const cx = w / 2;
  const cy = h / 2;

  // Pie chart
  const pie = d3.pie().value(d => d.share).sort(null);
  const arc = d3.arc().innerRadius(radius * 0.5).outerRadius(radius);

  const g = d3.select(svg).append('g')
    .attr('transform', `translate(${cx},${cy})`);

  g.selectAll('.alloc-slice')
    .data(pie(entries))
    .enter()
    .append('path')
    .attr('class', 'alloc-slice')
    .attr('d', arc)
    .attr('fill', (d, i) => COLORS[i % COLORS.length])
    .on('mouseenter', (event, d) => {
      showTooltip(event, `
        <strong>${d.data.id}</strong><br>
        Доля: ${(d.data.share * 100).toFixed(1)}%<br>
        Внимание: ${d.data.attention.toFixed(2)}<br>
        Momentum: ${d.data.momentum >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${d.data.momentum.toFixed(2)}<br>
        Velocity: ${d.data.velocity.toFixed(2)}
      `);
    })
    .on('mousemove', moveTooltip)
    .on('mouseleave', hideTooltip);

  // Labels для топ-5
  const top5 = pie(entries.slice(0, 5));
  const labelArc = d3.arc().innerRadius(radius * 0.75).outerRadius(radius * 0.75);

  g.selectAll('.alloc-label')
    .data(top5)
    .enter()
    .append('text')
    .attr('class', 'alloc-label')
    .attr('transform', d => `translate(${labelArc.centroid(d)})`)
    .attr('text-anchor', 'middle')
    .text(d => d.data.id.substring(0, 10));

  g.selectAll('.alloc-value')
    .data(top5)
    .enter()
    .append('text')
    .attr('class', 'alloc-value')
    .attr('transform', d => {
      const c = labelArc.centroid(d);
      return `translate(${c[0]},${c[1] + 12})`;
    })
    .attr('text-anchor', 'middle')
    .text(d => `${(d.data.share * 100).toFixed(0)}%`);
}

function renderExplosive(ad) {
  const list = $('explosive-list');
  const explosive = (ad.topExplosive || []).slice(0, 10);

  if (explosive.length === 0) {
    list.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">Нет данных</div>';
    return;
  }

  list.innerHTML = explosive.map(e => {
    const color = e.willExplode ? '#ef4444' :
                  e.explosiveness > 0.5 ? '#f97316' :
                  e.explosiveness > 0.2 ? '#eab308' : '#22c55e';
    return `
      <div class="explosive-item ${e.willExplode ? 'exploding' : ''}">
        <div class="explosive-header">
          <span class="explosive-topic">${e.topic}</span>
          <span class="explosive-score" style="color: ${color};">
            ${e.explosiveness.toFixed(2)}
          </span>
        </div>
        <div class="explosive-meta">
          attention: ${e.currentAttention.toFixed(1)} → ${e.predictedAttention.toFixed(1)}
          · velocity: ${e.velocity.toFixed(2)}
          ${e.willExplode ? ' · ⚡ EXPLODING' : ''}
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');
}

function renderTimeline(ad) {
  const svg = $('timeline-chart');
  const w = svg.clientWidth || 1200;
  const h = 350;
  svg.setAttribute('viewBox', `0 0 ${w} ${h}`);
  svg.innerHTML = '';

  // Извлекаем историю из топ-10 тем
  const topics = (ad.topExplosive || []).slice(0, 10).map(e => e.topic);
  if (topics.length === 0) {
    svg.innerHTML = `<text x="${w/2}" y="${h/2}" text-anchor="middle" fill="#5a6b8a" font-size="12">Нет данных</text>`;
    return;
  }

  // Проверяем, есть ли история в данных
  const hasHistory = ad.attentionAllocation && Object.values(ad.attentionAllocation).some(v => v.history);

  if (!hasHistory) {
    // Fallback: рисуем bars для текущего attention
    const barData = topics.map(t => ({
      topic: t,
      attention: ad.attentionAllocation[t]?.attention || 0,
    }));

    const margin = { top: 20, right: 30, bottom: 60, left: 60 };
    const pw = w - margin.left - margin.right;
    const ph = h - margin.top - margin.bottom;

    const maxA = Math.max(...barData.map(d => d.attention), 1);
    const xScale = d3.scaleBand().domain(topics).range([0, pw]).padding(0.2);
    const yScale = d3.scaleLinear().domain([0, maxA]).range([ph, 0]);

    const g = d3.select(svg).append('g').attr('transform', `translate(${margin.left},${margin.top})`);

    g.selectAll('.bar')
      .data(barData)
      .enter()
      .append('rect')
      .attr('x', d => xScale(d.topic))
      .attr('y', d => yScale(d.attention))
      .attr('width', xScale.bandwidth())
      .attr('height', d => ph - yScale(d.attention))
      .attr('fill', (d, i) => COLORS[i % COLORS.length])
      .attr('rx', 4);

    // Labels
    g.selectAll('.tl-label')
      .data(barData)
      .enter()
      .append('text')
      .attr('class', 'tl-axis')
      .attr('x', d => xScale(d.topic) + xScale.bandwidth() / 2)
      .attr('y', ph + 20)
      .attr('text-anchor', 'middle')
      .attr('font-size', '10')
      .text(d => d.topic.substring(0, 10));

    g.selectAll('.tl-value')
      .data(barData)
      .enter()
      .append('text')
      .attr('x', d => xScale(d.topic) + xScale.bandwidth() / 2)
      .attr('y', d => yScale(d.attention) - 6)
      .attr('text-anchor', 'middle')
      .attr('font-family', 'var(--mono)')
      .attr('font-size', '10')
      .attr('fill', 'var(--text)')
      .text(d => d.attention.toFixed(1));
  }
}

function renderShifts(ad) {
  const shifts = ad.shifts || [];
  const list = $('shifts-list');

  if (shifts.length === 0) {
    list.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">Нет обнаруженных сдвигов</div>';
    return;
  }

  list.innerHTML = shifts.map(s => `
    <div class="shift-item">
      <div class="shift-header">
        <span>
          <span class="shift-from">${s.from}</span>
          <span style="color: var(--text-faint);"> → </span>
          <span class="shift-to">${s.to}</span>
        </span>
        <span class="shift-magnitude">${s.magnitude.toFixed(2)}</span>
      </div>
      <div class="shift-desc">${s.interpretation}</div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function showTooltip(e, html) {
  const tt = $('tooltip');
  tt.innerHTML = html;
  tt.classList.add('visible');
  moveTooltip(e);
}
function moveTooltip(e) {
  const tt = $('tooltip');
  tt.style.left = `${Math.min(e.clientX + 14, window.innerWidth - tt.offsetWidth - 10)}px`;
  tt.style.top = `${Math.min(e.clientY + 14, window.innerHeight - tt.offsetHeight - 10)}px`;
}
function hideTooltip() { $('tooltip').classList.remove('visible'); }

load();
setInterval(load, CONFIG.refreshMs);

let resizeTimer;
window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
  clearTimeout(resizeTimer);
  resizeTimer = setTimeout(render, 300);
});
</script>

</body>
</html>

<script>
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const CONFIG = { dataUrl: '../runs/predictions/latest_forecast.json', refreshMs: 60000 };

const STRATEGY_COLORS = {
  escalate: '#ef4444',
  hold: '#eab308',
  deescalate: '#22c55e',
  deceive: '#8b5cf6',
  forecast_high: '#ef4444',
  forecast_low: '#22c55e',
  withhold: '#64748b',
};

const OPPONENT_COLORS = {
  russia: '#ef4444',
  china: '#f97316',
  usa: '#3b82f6',
  eu: '#22c55e',
  iran: '#8b5cf6',
  nato: '#06b6d4',
};

let DATA = null;

async function load() {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(CONFIG.dataUrl);
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
    DATA = await res.json();
    render();
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Load error:', e);
  }
}

function render() {
  if (!DATA) return;
  const ace = DATA.adversarialCoEvolution;
  if (!ace) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.empty').forEach(el => el.textContent = 'Нет данных о co-evolution');
    return;
  }

  renderKeyInsight(ace);
  renderOpponents(ace);
  renderHistory(ace);
}

function renderKeyInsight(ace) {
  const el = $('key-insight');
  const adapting = (ace.adaptations || []).filter(a => a.adaptation?.adapted);

  if (adapting.length > 0) {
    el.className = 'key-insight';
    el.innerHTML = `
      ⚠ <strong>${adapting.length}</strong> противник(ов) адаптируется к нашим прогнозам<br>
      <span style="font-size: 12px; font-weight: 400; margin-top: 8px; display: block;">
        ${adapting.map(a => `${a.opponent.toUpperCase()} (KL=${a.adaptation.klDivergence.toFixed(2)})`).join(', ')}
      </span>
    `;
  } else {
    el.className = 'key-insight safe';
    el.innerHTML = `
      ✓ Противники не демонстрируют явной адаптации<br>
      <span style="font-size: 12px; font-weight: 400; margin-top: 8px; display: block;">
        Текущая стратегия прогнозирования оптимальна
      </span>
    `;
  }
}

function renderOpponents(ace) {
  const grid = $('opponents-grid');
  const adaptations = ace.adaptations || [];

  $('opponents-count').textContent = `${adaptations.length}`;

  if (adaptations.length === 0) {
    grid.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">Нет моделей противников</div>';
    return;
  }

  grid.innerHTML = adaptations.map(a => {
    const oppColor = OPPONENT_COLORS[a.opponent] || '#64748b';
    const isAdapting = a.adaptation?.adapted;
    const distribution = a.distribution || {};

    const strategyRows = Object.entries(distribution)
      .sort((x, y) => y[1] - x[1])
      .map(([strategy, prob]) => {
        const color = STRATEGY_COLORS[strategy] || '#64748b';
        return `
          <div class="strategy-row">
            <span class="strategy-name">${strategy}</span>
            <div class="strategy-track">
              <div class="strategy-fill" style="width: ${prob * 100}%; background: ${color};"></div>
            </div>
            <span class="strategy-value" style="color: ${color};">${(prob * 100).toFixed(1)}%</span>
          </div>
        `;
      }).join('');

    return `
      <div class="opponent-card ${isAdapting ? 'adapting' : ''}" style="border-left-color: ${oppColor};">
        <div class="opponent-header">
          <span class="opponent-name" style="color: ${oppColor};">${a.opponent}</span>
          <span class="opponent-adapt-badge ${isAdapting ? 'yes' : 'no'}">
            ${isAdapting ? '⚠ Адаптируется' : '✓ Стабилен'}
          </span>
        </div>
        <div class="strategy-distribution">
          ${strategyRows}
        </div>
        <div class="predicted-next">
          <div class="predicted-next-label">Прогноз следующей стратегии</div>
          <div class="predicted-next-value" style="color: ${STRATEGY_COLORS[a.predictedStrategy] || oppColor};">
            ${a.predictedStrategy} (${(a.predictedProbability * 100).toFixed(1)}%)
          </div>
        </div>
        ${a.adaptation?.klDivergence !== undefined ? `
          <div class="kl-info">
            KL divergence: ${a.adaptation.klDivergence.toFixed(3)}
            ${isAdapting ? '(> 0.3 = адаптация)' : ''}
          </div>
        ` : ''}
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');
}

function renderHistory(ace) {
  const list = $('history-list');
  const history = ace.evolutionHistory || [];

  if (history.length === 0) {
    list.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">Нет истории симуляции</div>';
    return;
  }

  list.innerHTML = history.reverse().slice(0, 20).map((h, idx) => {
    const actionsHtml = (h.actions || []).map(a => `
      <div style="padding: 6px 8px; background: var(--surface3); border-radius: 6px; margin-bottom: 4px;">
        <div class="history-header">
          <span style="color: ${OPPONENT_COLORS[a.opponent] || 'var(--text)'};">${a.opponent}</span>
          <span>наблюдалось: <span class="history-observed">${a.observed}</span></span>
        </div>
        <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; font-size: 10px; color: var(--text-faint);">
          <span>прогноз: <span class="history-predicted">${a.predicted}</span></span>
          <span>${a.adaptation?.adapted ? '⚠ адаптация' : 'ok'}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    `).join('');

    return `
      <div class="history-item">
        <div class="history-header">
          <span style="color: var(--accent2); font-weight: 700;">Раунд #${h.round || idx + 1}</span>
          <span class="history-meta">${actionsHtml ? '' : '(нет действий)'}</span>
        </div>
        ${actionsHtml}
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');
}

function showTooltip(e, html) {
  const tt = $('tooltip');
  tt.innerHTML = html;
  tt.classList.add('visible');
  moveTooltip(e);
}
function moveTooltip(e) {
  const tt = $('tooltip');
  tt.style.left = `${Math.min(e.clientX + 14, window.innerWidth - tt.offsetWidth - 10)}px`;
  tt.style.top = `${Math.min(e.clientY + 14, window.innerHeight - tt.offsetHeight - 10)}px`;
}
function hideTooltip() { $('tooltip').classList.remove('visible'); }

load();
setInterval(load, CONFIG.refreshMs);

let resizeTimer;
window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
  clearTimeout(resizeTimer);
  resizeTimer = setTimeout(render, 300);
});
</script>

</body>
</html>

<script src="https://d3js.org/d3.v7.min.js"></script>
<script>
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const CONFIG = { dataUrl: '../runs/predictions/latest_forecast.json', refreshMs: 60000 };

const LAYER_COLORS = {
  cyber: '#ec4899',
  info: '#8b5cf6',
  finance: '#3b82f6',
  physical: '#22c55e',
};

let DATA = null;
let simulation = null;

async function load() {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(CONFIG.dataUrl);
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
    DATA = await res.json();
    render();
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Load error:', e);
  }
}

function render() {
  if (!DATA) return;
  const hg = DATA.hypergraphContagion;
  if (!hg) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.empty').forEach(el => el.textContent = 'Нет данных о гиперграфе');
    return;
  }

  renderStats(hg);
  renderHypergraph(hg);
  renderHyperedgesList(hg);
  renderUpdates(hg);
}

function renderStats(hg) {
  $('stat-nodes').textContent = hg.stats?.nodeCount || 0;
  $('stat-hyperedges').textContent = hg.stats?.hyperedgeCount || 0;
  $('stat-avg-size').textContent = (hg.stats?.avgHyperedgeSize || 0).toFixed(2);
  $('stat-critical').textContent = hg.criticalHyperedges?.length || 0;
}

function renderHypergraph(hg) {
  const svg = $('hypergraph-svg');
  const w = svg.clientWidth || 900;
  const h = 600;
  svg.setAttribute('viewBox', `0 0 ${w} ${h}`);
  svg.innerHTML = '';

  const nodes = hg.hyperedges ? extractNodesFromHyperedges(hg.hyperedges) : [];
  const edges = hg.hyperedges || [];

  if (nodes.length === 0) {
    svg.innerHTML = `<text x="${w/2}" y="${h/2}" text-anchor="middle" fill="#5a6b8a" font-size="12">Нет данных</text>`;
    return;
  }

  // D3 simulation
  simulation = d3.forceSimulation(nodes)
    .force('charge', d3.forceManyBody().strength(-400))
    .force('center', d3.forceCenter(w / 2, h / 2))
    .force('collision', d3.forceCollide().radius(d => 30 + (d.currentProb || 0.5) * 20))
    .force('link', d3.forceLink(
      // Связи: для каждого гиперребра соединяем все пары
      buildPairwiseLinks(edges)
    ).id(d => d.id).distance(150).strength(0.3));

  const g = d3.select(svg).append('g');

  // Hyperedges как многоугольники (для 3+ узлов)
  const hyperedgeGroups = g.selectAll('.hyperedge-group')
    .data(edges.filter(e => e.nodes.length <= 5)) // для >5 рисуем как линии
    .enter()
    .append('g')
    .attr('class', 'hyperedge-group');

  // Линии для рёбер > 3 узлов (упрощённо)
  const links = g.selectAll('.hg-edge')
    .data(buildPairwiseLinks(edges))
    .enter()
    .append('line')
    .attr('class', d => `hg-edge ${d.type}`)
    .attr('stroke', d => {
      const color = { AND: '#ef4444', OR: '#3b82f6', MAJORITY: '#eab308' }[d.type];
      return color || '#64748b';
    })
    .attr('stroke-width', d => Math.min(5, 1 + (d.weight || 1) * 1.5))
    .attr('stroke-opacity', d => d.active ? 0.9 : 0.3);

  // Узлы
  const nodeGroups = g.selectAll('.hg-node')
    .data(nodes)
    .enter()
    .append('g')
    .attr('class', 'hg-node')
    .call(d3.drag()
      .on('start', dragstarted)
      .on('drag', dragged)
      .on('end', dragended));

  nodeGroups.append('circle')
    .attr('class', 'hg-node-circle')
    .attr('r', d => 24 + (d.currentProb || 0.5) * 16)
    .attr('fill', d => {
      const color = LAYER_COLORS[d.layer] || '#64748b';
      const alpha = Math.floor((d.currentProb || 0.5) * 100 + 30).toString(16).padStart(2, '0');
      return `${color}${alpha}`;
    })
    .attr('stroke', d => LAYER_COLORS[d.layer] || '#64748b')
    .on('mouseenter', (event, d) => {
      showTooltip(event, `
        <strong>${d.name || d.id}</strong><br>
        Слой: ${d.layer || '—'}<br>
        Вероятность: ${((d.currentProb || 0.5) * 100).toFixed(1)}%
      `);
    })
    .on('mousemove', moveTooltip)
    .on('mouseleave', hideTooltip);

  nodeGroups.append('text')
    .attr('class', 'hg-node-label')
    .attr('dy', '-4')
    .text(d => (d.name || d.id).substring(0, 14));

  nodeGroups.append('text')
    .attr('class', 'hg-node-prob')
    .attr('dy', '12')
    .attr('fill', d => LAYER_COLORS[d.layer] || '#64748b')
    .text(d => `${((d.currentProb || 0.5) * 100).toFixed(0)}%`);

  // Update positions on tick
  simulation.on('tick', () => {
    links
      .attr('x1', d => d.source.x)
      .attr('y1', d => d.source.y)
      .attr('x2', d => d.target.x)
      .attr('y2', d => d.target.y);

    nodeGroups.attr('transform', d => `translate(${d.x},${d.y})`);
  });

  function dragstarted(event, d) {
    if (!event.active) simulation.alphaTarget(0.3).restart();
    d.fx = d.x;
    d.fy = d.y;
  }
  function dragged(event, d) {
    d.fx = event.x;
    d.fy = event.y;
  }
  function dragended(event, d) {
    if (!event.active) simulation.alphaTarget(0);
    d.fx = null;
    d.fy = null;
  }
}

function extractNodesFromHyperedges(hyperedges) {
  const nodeMap = new Map();
  for (const he of hyperedges) {
    for (const nodeId of he.nodes) {
      if (!nodeMap.has(nodeId)) {
        nodeMap.set(nodeId, {
          id: nodeId,
          name: nodeId.replace(/_/g, ' '),
          layer: inferLayer(nodeId),
          currentProb: 0.5,
        });
      }
    }
  }
  return [...nodeMap.values()];
}

function inferLayer(nodeId) {
  if (/cyber|ransomware|ddos|data.?leak/i.test(nodeId)) return 'cyber';
  if (/disinfo|narrative|propaganda|media|info/i.test(nodeId)) return 'info';
  if (/vix|market|sanction|currency|credit|capital/i.test(nodeId)) return 'finance';
  if (/conflict|military|naval|radiation|physical|satellite/i.test(nodeId)) return 'physical';
  return 'unknown';
}

function buildPairwiseLinks(edges) {
  const links = [];
  for (const he of edges) {
    for (let i = 0; i < he.nodes.length; i++) {
      for (let j = i + 1; j < he.nodes.length; j++) {
        links.push({
          source: he.nodes[i],
          target: he.nodes[j],
          type: he.type,
          weight: he.weight,
          hyperedgeId: he.id,
          description: he.description,
        });
      }
    }
  }
  return links;
}

function renderHyperedgesList(hg) {
  const list = $('hyperedge-list');
  const edges = hg.hyperedges || [];
  $('hyperedge-count').textContent = `${edges.length}`;

  if (edges.length === 0) {
    list.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">Нет гиперрёбер</div>';
    return;
  }

  list.innerHTML = edges.map(he => `
    <div class="hyperedge-item ${he.type}">
      <div class="hyperedge-header">
        <span class="hyperedge-nodes">${he.nodes.join(' + ')}</span>
        <span class="hyperedge-weight">×${he.weight.toFixed(1)}</span>
      </div>
      <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center;">
        <span class="hyperedge-type">${he.type}</span>
        <span style="font-size: 10px; color: var(--text-faint); font-family: var(--mono);">
          lag ${he.lagHours}ч
        </span>
      </div>
      ${he.description ? `<div class="hyperedge-desc">${he.description}</div>` : ''}
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function renderUpdates(hg) {
  const updates = hg.updates || [];
  $('updates-count').textContent = `${updates.length}`;

  const list = $('updates-list');
  if (updates.length === 0) {
    list.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">Нет активных обновлений</div>';
    return;
  }

  const sorted = [...updates].sort((a, b) => Math.abs(b.delta) - Math.abs(a.delta));

  list.innerHTML = sorted.slice(0, 20).map(u => {
    const color = u.delta > 0 ? '#ef4444' : '#22c55e';
    return `
      <div style="padding: 10px 12px; background: var(--surface2); border-radius: 8px; border-left: 3px solid ${color};">
        <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin-bottom: 4px;">
          <span style="font-weight: 700; font-size: 12px;">${u.target}</span>
          <span style="font-family: var(--mono); font-size: 12px; color: ${color}; font-weight: 700;">
            ${u.delta > 0 ? '+' : ''}${(u.delta * 100).toFixed(1)}%
          </span>
        </div>
        <div style="font-size: 10px; color: var(--text-faint); margin-bottom: 4px;">
          источники: ${u.sources.join(', ')}
        </div>
        <div style="font-size: 10px; color: var(--text-faint); font-family: var(--mono);">
          type: ${u.type} · activation: ${(u.activation * 100).toFixed(0)}%
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');
}

function showTooltip(e, html) {
  const tt = $('tooltip');
  tt.innerHTML = html;
  tt.classList.add('visible');
  moveTooltip(e);
}
function moveTooltip(e) {
  const tt = $('tooltip');
  tt.style.left = `${Math.min(e.clientX + 14, window.innerWidth - tt.offsetWidth - 10)}px`;
  tt.style.top = `${Math.min(e.clientY + 14, window.innerHeight - tt.offsetHeight - 10)}px`;
}
function hideTooltip() { $('tooltip').classList.remove('visible'); }

load();
setInterval(load, CONFIG.refreshMs);

let resizeTimer;
window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
  clearTimeout(resizeTimer);
  resizeTimer = setTimeout(render, 300);
});
</script>

</body>
</html>

<div class="header">
  <h1>Crucix <span>· Plugin Manager</span></h1>
  <div class="header-actions">
    <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="reloadAll()">Reload All</button>
    <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="showAvailable()">+ Install New</button>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="stats-grid">
  <div class="stat-card">
    <div class="stat-value" id="stat-installed">—</div>
    <div class="stat-label">Installed</div>
  </div>
  <div class="stat-card">
    <div class="stat-value" id="stat-enabled">—</div>
    <div class="stat-label">Enabled</div>
  </div>
  <div class="stat-card">
    <div class="stat-value" id="stat-hooks">—</div>
    <div class="stat-label">Active Hooks</div>
  </div>
  <div class="stat-card">
    <div class="stat-value" id="stat-available">—</div>
    <div class="stat-label">Available</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="tabs">
  <div class="tab active" data-tab="installed" onclick="switchTab('installed')">Installed</div>
  <div class="tab" data-tab="available" onclick="switchTab('available')">Available</div>
</div>

<div id="content">
  <div class="loading">
    <div class="spinner"></div>
    Loading plugins...
  </div>
</div>

<div class="modal-overlay" id="config-modal">
  <div class="modal">
    <div class="modal-header">
      <div class="modal-title" id="modal-title">Plugin Config</div>
      <button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button>
    </div>
    <div id="modal-content"></div>
    <div class="modal-actions">
      <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button>
      <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="saveConfig()">Save</button>
    </div>
  </div>

<script>
let currentTab = 'installed';
let currentPlugin = null;
let data = { installed: [], available: [], status: {} };

async function load() {
  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/plugins');
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
    data = await res.json();
    render();
  } catch (e) {
    document.getElementById('content').innerHTML = `
      <div class="empty">
        <div class="empty-icon">Error</div>
        <div>Error loading plugins: ${e.message}</div>
        <button class="btn btn-primary" style="margin-top: 16px;" onclick="load()">Retry</button>
      </div>
    `;
  }
}

function render() {
  renderStats();
  renderContent();
}

function renderStats() {
  document.getElementById('stat-installed').textContent = data.installed?.length || 0;
  document.getElementById('stat-enabled').textContent = (data.installed || []).filter(p => p.enabled).length;
  document.getElementById('stat-hooks').textContent = Object.keys(data.status?.hooks?.hooks || {}).length;
  document.getElementById('stat-available').textContent = (data.available || []).filter(p => !p.isInstalled).length;
}

function renderContent() {
  if (currentTab === 'installed') renderInstalled();
  else renderAvailable();
}

function renderInstalled() {
  const plugins = data.installed || [];
  if (plugins.length === 0) {
    document.getElementById('content').innerHTML = `
      <div class="empty">
        <div class="empty-icon">Empty</div>
        <div>Нет установленных плагинов</div>
        <button class="btn btn-primary" style="margin-top: 16px;" onclick="switchTab('available')">Browse Available</button>
      </div>
    `;
    return;
  }
  const html = `
    <table class="plugins-table">
      <thead><tr>
        <th>Plugin</th><th>Type</th><th>Hooks</th><th>Status</th><th>Enabled</th><th>Actions</th>
      </tr></thead>
      <tbody>${plugins.map(p => renderPluginRow(p)).join('')}</tbody>
    </table>
  `;
  document.getElementById('content').innerHTML = html;
}

function renderPluginRow(p) {
  return `
    <tr>
      <td><div class="plugin-name">
        <span class="plugin-icon">${getPluginIcon(p.type)}</span>
        <div><div>${p.name}</div><div class="plugin-version">v${p.version}</div></div>
      </div></td>
      <td><span class="tag tag-${p.type}">${p.type}</span></td>
      <td><div class="plugin-tags">
        ${(p.hooks || []).slice(0, 3).map(h => `<span class="tag" style="background: var(--surface3); color: var(--text-dim);">${h}</span>`).join('')}
      </div></td>
      <td><span class="status-badge ${p.enabled ? 'status-enabled' : 'status-disabled'}">${p.enabled ? 'Active' : 'Disabled'}</span></td>
      <td>
        <label class="toggle">
          <input type="checkbox" ${p.enabled ? 'checked' : ''} onchange="togglePlugin('${p.name}', this.checked)">
          <span class="toggle-slider"></span>
        </label>
      </td>
      <td><div class="actions">
        <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-small" onclick="openConfig('${p.name}')">Cfg</button>
        <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-small" onclick="showLogs('${p.name}')">Log</button>
        <button class="btn btn-danger btn-small" onclick="uninstall('${p.name}')">Del</button>
      </div></td>
    </tr>
  `;
}

function renderAvailable() {
  const plugins = (data.available || []).filter(p => !p.isInstalled);
  if (plugins.length === 0) {
    document.getElementById('content').innerHTML = `<div class="empty"><div class="empty-icon">OK</div><div>Все доступные плагины установлены</div></div>`;
    return;
  }
  const html = `
    <table class="plugins-table">
      <thead><tr><th>Plugin</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th><th>Author</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead>
      <tbody>${plugins.map(p => `
        <tr>
          <td><div class="plugin-name">
            <span class="plugin-icon">${getPluginIcon(p.type)}</span>
            <div><div>${p.name}</div>${p.official ? '<span class="tag tag-official">OFFICIAL</span>' : ''}</div>
          </div></td>
          <td><span class="tag tag-${p.type}">${p.type}</span></td>
          <td class="plugin-desc" title="${p.description}">${p.description}</td>
          <td style="color: var(--text-dim); font-size: 12px;">${p.author}</td>
          <td><button class="btn btn-primary btn-small" onclick="install('${p.name}')">Install</button></td>
        </tr>
      `).join('')}</tbody>
    </table>
  `;
  document.getElementById('content').innerHTML = html;
}

function getPluginIcon(type) {
  return { signal: 'Sig', notifier: 'Not', fetcher: 'Fet', analyzer: 'Ana', full: 'Full' }[type] || 'Plg';
}

async function install(name) {
  toast(`Installing ${name}...`, 'info');
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`/api/plugins/${name}/install`, { method: 'POST' });
    const result = await res.json();
    if (result.ok) { toast(`Installed ${name}`, 'success'); await load(); }
    else toast(`Failed: ${result.error || 'unknown'}`, 'error');
  } catch (e) { toast(`Error: ${e.message}`, 'error'); }
}

async function uninstall(name) {
  if (!confirm(`Uninstall ${name}?`)) return;
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`/api/plugins/${name}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    const result = await res.json();
    if (result.ok) { toast(`Uninstalled ${name}`, 'success'); await load(); }
    else toast(`Failed: ${result.error}`, 'error');
  } catch (e) { toast(`Error: ${e.message}`, 'error'); }
}

async function togglePlugin(name, enabled) {
  const action = enabled ? 'enable' : 'disable';
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`/api/plugins/${name}/${action}`, { method: 'POST' });
    const result = await res.json();
    if (result.ok) { toast(`${enabled ? 'Enabled' : 'Disabled'} ${name}`, 'success'); await load(); }
    else toast(`Failed: ${result.error}`, 'error');
  } catch (e) { toast(`Error: ${e.message}`, 'error'); }
}

async function reloadAll() {
  toast('Reloading all plugins...', 'info');
  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/plugins/reload', { method: 'POST' });
    const result = await res.json();
    toast(`Reloaded ${result.reloaded} plugins`, 'success');
    await load();
  } catch (e) { toast(`Error: ${e.message}`, 'error'); }
}

async function openConfig(name) {
  currentPlugin = name;
  document.getElementById('modal-title').textContent = `${name} · Configuration`;
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`/api/plugins/${name}/config`);
    const config = await res.json();
    document.getElementById('modal-content').innerHTML = `
      <p style="color: var(--text-dim); font-size: 13px; margin-bottom: 12px;">Edit plugin configuration as JSON.</p>
      <textarea class="config-editor" id="config-editor">${JSON.stringify(config, null, 2)}</textarea>
    `;
    document.getElementById('config-modal').classList.add('active');
  } catch (e) { toast(`Error: ${e.message}`, 'error'); }
}

async function saveConfig() {
  if (!currentPlugin) return;
  try {
    const editor = document.getElementById('config-editor');
    const config = JSON.parse(editor.value);
    const res = await fetch(`/api/plugins/${currentPlugin}/config`, {
      method: 'PUT',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(config),
    });
    const result = await res.json();
    if (result.ok) { toast('Config saved', 'success'); closeModal(); await load(); }
    else toast(`Failed: ${result.error}`, 'error');
  } catch (e) { toast(`Invalid JSON: ${e.message}`, 'error'); }
}

function closeModal() {
  document.getElementById('config-modal').classList.remove('active');
  currentPlugin = null;
}

async function showLogs(name) {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`/api/plugins/${name}/logs?limit=50`);
    const { lines, total } = await res.json();
    const content = lines.length > 0 ? lines.join('\n') : '(no logs)';
    document.getElementById('modal-title').textContent = `${name} · Logs (${total} lines)`;
    document.getElementById('modal-content').innerHTML = `
      <pre style="background: #000; color: #22c55e; padding: 16px; border-radius: 8px; font-family: var(--mono); font-size: 12px; max-height: 400px; overflow: auto; line-height: 1.5;">${escapeHtml(content)}</pre>
    `;
    document.getElementById('config-modal').classList.add('active');
    currentPlugin = null;
  } catch (e) { toast(`Error: ${e.message}`, 'error'); }
}

function switchTab(tab) {
  currentTab = tab;
  document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(t => t.classList.toggle('active', t.dataset.tab === tab));
  renderContent();
}

function showAvailable() { switchTab('available'); }

function escapeHtml(str) {
  return String(str).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
}

function toast(message, type = 'info') {
  const el = document.createElement('div');
  el.className = `toast ${type}`;
  el.textContent = message;
  document.body.appendChild(el);
  setTimeout(() => {
    el.style.animation = 'slideIn 0.3s ease reverse';
    setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 300);
  }, 3000);
}

load();
setInterval(load, 30000);
</script>

</body>
</html>

<script>
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const COLORS = {low:'#06b6d4',moderate:'#22c55e',elevated:'#eab308',high:'#f97316',critical:'#ef4444'};

function sigColor(v) { return v > 0.7 ? '#ef4444' : v > 0.5 ? '#f97316' : v > 0.3 ? '#eab308' : v > 0.15 ? '#22c55e' : '#06b6d4'; }

async function load() {
try {
const res = await fetch('../runs/predictions/latest_forecast.json');
if (!res.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + res.status);
const data = await res.json();
render(data.compositeRisk);
} catch (e) {
document.querySelectorAll('.empty').forEach(el => el.textContent = 'Нет данных: ' + e.message);
}
}

function render(cr) {
if (!cr) return;
const value = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, cr.composite || 0));
const level = cr.level || 'unknown';
const color = COLORS[level] || '#64748b';

$('composite-value').textContent = value.toFixed(3);
$('composite-value').style.color = color;
$('composite-level').textContent = level;
$('composite-level').style.color = color;
$('composite-meta').textContent = 'confidence: ' + (cr.confidence || '—') + ' · signals: ' + ((cr.signals || []).length);

const circ = 2 * Math.PI * 78;
const offset = circ * (1 - value);
const fill = $('orb-fill');
fill.setAttribute('stroke', color);
fill.setAttribute('stroke-dashoffset', offset);

$('scale-marker').style.left = (value * 100) + '%';

if (cr.topDrivers && cr.topDrivers.length > 0) {
$('drivers').innerHTML = cr.topDrivers.map(d => {
const c = sigColor(d.value);
return '<div class="driver" style="border-left-color:' + c + '">' +
'<div class="driver-head"><span class="driver-name">' + d.name + '</span>' +
'<span class="driver-value" style="color:' + c + '">' + (d.value * 100).toFixed(1) + '%</span></div>' +
'<div class="driver-rationale">' + (d.rationale || '') + '</div></div>';
}).join('');
}

if (cr.signals && cr.signals.length > 0) {
const sorted = [...cr.signals].sort((a, b) => (b.value * b.weight) - (a.value * a.weight));
$('signals-body').innerHTML = sorted.map((s, i) => {
const c = sigColor(s.value);
return '<tr>' +
'<td><strong>' + s.name + '</strong></td>' +
'<td><span class="sig-bar"><span class="sig-bar-fill" style="width:' + (s.value * 100) + '%;background:' + c + '"></span></span>' +
'<span style="font-family:var(--mono);font-weight:700;color:' + c + '">' + (s.value * 100).toFixed(1) + '%</span></td>' +
'<td style="font-family:var(--mono);color:var(--accent)">×' + (s.weight || 1).toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
'<td style="color:var(--text-faint);font-size:11px">' + (s.rationale || '') + '</td></tr>';
}).join('');
}
}

<script>
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const state = { ws: null, events: [], maxEvents: 200 };

function connect() {
const url = $('ws-url').value;
setStatus('connecting', 'подключение...');
try { state.ws = new WebSocket(url); } catch (e) { setStatus('error', 'ошибка'); return; }
state.ws.onopen = () => { setStatus('connected', 'подключено'); state.ws.send(JSON.stringify({action:'subscribe',filters:{}})); };
state.ws.onmessage = (e) => { try { const msg = JSON.parse(e.data); handleMessage(msg); } catch {} };
state.ws.onclose = () => { setStatus('error', 'отключено'); setTimeout(connect, 3000); };
state.ws.onerror = () => { setStatus('error', 'ошибка'); };
}

function setStatus(type, text) {
$('status-dot').className = 'dot' + (type === 'connected' ? ' connected' : '');
$('status-text').textContent = text;
}

function handleMessage(msg) {
if (msg.type === 'welcome' || msg.type === 'pong' || msg.type === 'subscribed' || msg.type === 'stats') return;
state.events.unshift(msg);
if (state.events.length > state.maxEvents) state.events.pop();
render();
}

function render() {
$('event-count').textContent = state.events.length + ' событий';
const stream = $('stream');
if (state.events.length === 0) { stream.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">Ожидание событий...</div>'; return; }
stream.innerHTML = state.events.slice(0, 50).map(renderEvent).join('');
}

function renderEvent(e) {
const time = new Date(e.serverTimestamp || Date.now()).toLocaleTimeString('ru-RU');
let className = 'event';
if (e.type === 'alert') className += ' alert-' + e.severity;
let typeLabel = ({prediction:'Прогноз', alert:'Алерт', signal_high:'Сигнал', regime_change:'Смена режима'})[e.type] || e.type;
let body = '';
if (e.type === 'prediction') body = 'Composite: <strong>' + (e.data?.compositeRisk?.composite?.toFixed(3) || '—') + '</strong>';
else if (e.type === 'alert') body = 'Composite: <strong>' + (e.data?.composite?.toFixed(3) || '—') + '</strong>';
else if (e.type === 'signal_high') body = '<strong>' + (e.data?.name || '') + '</strong> = ' + ((e.data?.value || 0) * 100).toFixed(1) + '%';
return '<div class="' + className + '"><div class="event-header"><span class="event-type">' + typeLabel + '</span><span class="event-time">' + time + '</span></div><div class="event-body">' + body + '</div></div>';
}

function reconnect() { if (state.ws) state.ws.close(); connect(); }

connect();
</script>

</body>
</html>

// dashboard/pwa/install.js
// Логика установки PWA + регистрация SW + push subscriptions

class PWAManager {
  constructor() {
    this.deferredPrompt = null;
    this.installed = false;
    this.swRegistration = null;
    this.subscription = null;

    this._setup();
  }

  _setup() {
    if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
      window.addEventListener('load', () => this.registerSW());
    }

    window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      this.deferredPrompt = e;
      this.showInstallBanner();
    });

    window.addEventListener('appinstalled', () => {
      this.installed = true;
      this.deferredPrompt = null;
      this.hideInstallBanner();
      console.log('[PWA] App installed');
    });

    if (window.matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)').matches) {
      this.installed = true;
      console.log('[PWA] Running in standalone mode');
    }
  }

  async registerSW() {
    try {
      const registration = await navigator.serviceWorker.register('/pwa/service-worker.js', {
        scope: '/',
      });

      this.swRegistration = registration;
      console.log('[PWA] SW registered:', registration.scope);

      registration.addEventListener('updatefound', () => {
        const newWorker = registration.installing;
        newWorker.addEventListener('statechange', () => {
          if (newWorker.state === 'installed' && navigator.serviceWorker.controller) {
            this.showUpdateBanner();
          }
        });
      });

      return registration;
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('[PWA] SW registration failed:', e);
      return null;
    }
  }

  async promptInstall() {
    if (!this.deferredPrompt) {
      console.log('[PWA] No install prompt available');
      return { ok: false, reason: 'no_prompt' };
    }

    this.deferredPrompt.prompt();
    const { outcome } = await this.deferredPrompt.userChoice;
    console.log('[PWA] User choice:', outcome);

    this.deferredPrompt = null;
    this.hideInstallBanner();

    return { ok: outcome === 'accepted', outcome };
  }

  showInstallBanner() {
    if (document.getElementById('pwa-install-banner')) return;

    const banner = document.createElement('div');
    banner.id = 'pwa-install-banner';
    banner.innerHTML = `
      <div style="
        position: fixed; bottom: 20px; left: 50%; transform: translateX(-50%);
        background: linear-gradient(135deg, #3b82f6, #8b5cf6);
        color: white; padding: 16px 24px; border-radius: 12px;
        box-shadow: 0 8px 32px rgba(0,0,0,0.3); z-index: 9999;
        display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 16px;
        font-family: -apple-system, sans-serif; max-width: 90%;
      ">
        <div style="flex: 1;">
          <div style="font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 4px;">Install Crucix</div>
          <div style="font-size: 12px; opacity: 0.9;">Get real-time alerts on your device</div>
        </div>
        <button id="pwa-install-btn" style="
          background: white; color: #3b82f6; border: none;
          padding: 8px 16px; border-radius: 6px; font-weight: 600;
          cursor: pointer; font-size: 13px;
        ">Install</button>
        <button id="pwa-dismiss-btn" style="
          background: transparent; color: white; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.3);
          width: 32px; height: 32px; border-radius: 6px; cursor: pointer;
          font-size: 18px; line-height: 1;
        ">x</button>
      </div>
    `;
    document.body.appendChild(banner);

    document.getElementById('pwa-install-btn').onclick = () => this.promptInstall();
    document.getElementById('pwa-dismiss-btn').onclick = () => this.hideInstallBanner();
  }

  hideInstallBanner() {
    const banner = document.getElementById('pwa-install-banner');
    if (banner) banner.remove();
  }

  showUpdateBanner() {
    const banner = document.createElement('div');
    banner.id = 'pwa-update-banner';
    banner.innerHTML = `
      <div style="
        position: fixed; top: 20px; left: 50%; transform: translateX(-50%);
        background: #1e293b; color: white; padding: 12px 20px;
        border-radius: 8px; box-shadow: 0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.4);
        z-index: 9999; display: flex; gap: 12px; align-items: center;
        font-family: -apple-system, sans-serif; font-size: 14px;
      ">
        <span>New version available</span>
        <button onclick="this.parentElement.parentElement.remove(); location.reload();" style="
          background: #3b82f6; color: white; border: none;
          padding: 6px 12px; border-radius: 6px; cursor: pointer;
        ">Reload</button>
      </div>
    `;
    document.body.appendChild(banner);
    setTimeout(() => banner.remove(), 30000);
  }

  async requestPushPermission() {
    if (!('Notification' in window)) {
      return { ok: false, reason: 'notifications_not_supported' };
    }

    if (Notification.permission === 'granted') {
      return await this.subscribePush();
    }

    if (Notification.permission === 'denied') {
      return { ok: false, reason: 'permission_denied' };
    }

    const permission = await Notification.requestPermission();
    if (permission !== 'granted') {
      return { ok: false, reason: 'permission_denied' };
    }

    return await this.subscribePush();
  }

  async subscribePush() {
    if (!this.swRegistration) {
      return { ok: false, reason: 'no_sw' };
    }

    try {
      const vapidPublicKey = await this._getVapidKey();

      if (!vapidPublicKey) {
        return { ok: false, reason: 'no_vapid_key' };
      }

      const subscription = await this.swRegistration.pushManager.subscribe({
        userVisibleOnly: true,
        applicationServerKey: this._urlBase64ToUint8Array(vapidPublicKey),
      });

      this.subscription = subscription;

      await this._sendSubscriptionToServer(subscription);

      return { ok: true, subscription };
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('[PWA] Push subscription failed:', e);
      return { ok: false, reason: e.message };
    }
  }

  async unsubscribePush() {
    if (!this.subscription) return { ok: true };

    try {
      await this.subscription.unsubscribe();
      await fetch('/api/push/unsubscribe', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ endpoint: this.subscription.endpoint }),
      });
      this.subscription = null;
      return { ok: true };
    } catch (e) {
      return { ok: false, reason: e.message };
    }
  }

  async _getVapidKey() {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/push/vapid-key');
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        return data.publicKey;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // Fallback
    }
    return null;
  }

  async _sendSubscriptionToServer(subscription) {
    try {
      await fetch('/api/push/subscribe', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(subscription),
      });
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('[PWA] Failed to send subscription:', e);
    }
  }

  _urlBase64ToUint8Array(base64String) {
    const padding = '='.repeat((4 - base64String.length % 4) % 4);
    const base64 = (base64String + padding).replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/');
    const rawData = window.atob(base64);
    const outputArray = new Uint8Array(rawData.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < rawData.length; ++i) {
      outputArray[i] = rawData.charCodeAt(i);
    }
    return outputArray;
  }

  async testNotification() {
    if (!this.swRegistration) return;
    await this.swRegistration.showNotification('Crucix Test', {
      body: 'Push notifications are working!',
      icon: '/pwa/icons/icon-192.png',
      badge: '/pwa/icons/icon-96.png',
      tag: 'test',
    });
  }

  getStatus() {
    return {
      installed: this.installed,
      swRegistered: !!this.swRegistration,
      pushSupported: 'PushManager' in window,
      notificationPermission: typeof Notification !== 'undefined' ? Notification.permission : 'unsupported',
      subscription: !!this.subscription,
    };
  }
}

window.crucixPWA = new PWAManager();

console.log('[PWA] Manager initialized');

export { PWAManager };

SIZE: 9468 bytes
SHA1: 9123ea65c1e142e0
